    rules_version = '2';
    service cloud.firestore {
      match /databases/{database}/documents {
        function signedIn() {
          // Suspension is an Auth control: admin suspension callables disable the
          // user, set this claim, and revoke refresh tokens. Reading users/{uid}
          // inside this foundational helper multiplies across nested predicates
          // and can exceed Firestore's 1,000-expression evaluation limit.
          return request.auth != null &&
            request.auth.token.get('suspended', false) != true;
        }
    
        function claimedRole() {
          return signedIn()
            ? request.auth.token.get('role', request.auth.token.get('userRole', request.auth.token.get('primaryRole', '')))
            : '';
        }
    
        function isNotSuspended() {
          return signedIn() && (
            !exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)) ||
            get(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)).data.get('suspended', false) != true
          );
        }
    
        function hasAdminClaimCycleFree() {
          return signedIn() && (
            (
              claimedRole() == '' &&
              (
                ('admin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('admin', false) == true) ||
                ('isAdmin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('isAdmin', false) == true)

          return signedIn() &&
            request.auth.token.get('email_verified', false) == true &&
            authEmail() != null &&

        }
    
        function emailOwnsCycleFree(data) {
          return emailMatchesCycleFree(data.get('email', null)) ||
            emailMatchesCycleFree(data.get('ownerEmail', null)) ||

        }
    
        function hasAdminClaim() {
          return signedIn() && (
            (
              claimedRole() == '' &&
              (
                ('admin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('admin', false) == true) ||
                ('isAdmin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('isAdmin', false) == true)
              )
            ) ||
            ('superAdmin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('superAdmin', false) == true) ||

        function isAdmin() {
          // Firestore profile fields are display/cache data, never an authorization

          return hasAdminClaim();

        function hasPermission(permission) {
          return signedIn() && 'permissions' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.permissions.get(permission, false) == true;
        }
    
        function canManageProperties() { return isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageProperties'); }
        function canManageContracts() { return isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageContracts'); }
        function canManageCompanyProfile() { return isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageCompanyProfile'); }
        function canReadUserDirectory() {
          return signedIn() && (

          return signedIn() && (

          );
        }
    
        function hasNonAdminDispatchClaimOnly() {
          return signedIn() && (
            hasPermission('canDispatchJobs') ||
            claimedRole() in ['operations_admin', 'operations_manager', 'dispatcher']
          );
        }
    
        function canDispatchJobs() {
          // Check inexpensive signed claims first. The database-backed suspension
          // lookup runs only for callers that actually possess dispatch authority.
          return hasDispatchAuthorityClaimOnly() && isNotSuspended();
        }
    
        function authEmail() { return signedIn() ? request.auth.token.get('email', null) : null; }
        function emailMatches(value) {
          return signedIn() &&

            authEmail() != null &&
            value != null &&
            value == authEmail();
        }
        function isOwnerId(ownerId) { return signedIn() && ownerId != null && ownerId == request.auth.uid; }
        function isOwner(ownerId) { return isOwnerId(ownerId); }
        function isTenantId(tenantId) { return signedIn() && tenantId != null && tenantId == request.auth.uid; }
        function isTechnicianId(techId) { return signedIn() && techId != null && techId == request.auth.uid; }
    
        function getTenantPropertyId() {
          return exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)) ? get(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)).data.get('propertyId', null) : null;
        }
        function isUnitTenant(unitId) {
          return signedIn() &&
            unitId is string &&
            exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(unitId)) && (
              get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(unitId)).data.get('tenantId', null) == request.auth.uid ||

        }

        }
    
        function owns(data) {

        }
    
        function tenantOwns(data) {
          return isTenantId(data.get('tenantId', null)) || isTenantId(data.get('tenantUid', null)) || isTenantId(data.get('authUid', null)) || isTenantId(data.get('userId', null)) || isTenantId(data.get('createdByUid', null));
        }
    
        function techOwns(data) {
          return isTechnicianId(data.get('assignedTechnicianId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('technicianId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('techId', null));
        }
    
        function brokerOwns(data) {
          return signedIn() && (
            data.get('brokerId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('brokerUid', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('userId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('createdByUid', null) == request.auth.uid
          );
        }
    
        function emailOwns(data) {
          return emailMatches(data.get('email', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('ownerEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('tenantEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('technicianEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('staffEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('brokerEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('recipientEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('contactEmail', null));
        }
    
        function ownerCanRead(data) { return signedIn() && (owns(data) || emailOwns(data)); }
        function staffCanRead(data) { return signedIn() && (techOwns(data) || isTechnicianId(data.get('uid', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('userId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('technicianId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('staffId', null)) || emailOwns(data)); }
        function staffCanCreate(data) { return signedIn() && (isTechnicianId(data.get('uid', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('userId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('technicianId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('staffId', null)) || emailOwns(data)); }
        function docPropertyId(data) { return data.get('propertyId', null); }
        function docUnitId(data) { return data.get('unitId', null); }
        function tenantUidOwns(data) { return isTenantId(data.get('tenantUid', null)); }
        function propertyScopedRead(data) {
          return signedIn() && (
            isAdmin() ||
            (docPropertyId(data) != null && isPropertyOwner(docPropertyId(data))) ||
            (docPropertyId(data) != null && getTenantPropertyId() == docPropertyId(data))
          );
        }
        function propertyScopedOwnerWrite(data) {
          return isAdmin() || (docPropertyId(data) != null && isPropertyOwner(docPropertyId(data)));
        }
        function isConfidentialRequest(data) {
          return ('requestType' in data && data.requestType == 'manager_issue') || ('category' in data && data.category == 'confidential');
        }
    
        function hasTechnicianClaim() {
          return signedIn() && (
            ('role' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.role == 'technician') ||
            ('userRole' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.userRole == 'technician') ||
            ('primaryRole' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.primaryRole == 'technician')
          );
        }
    
        function approvedTechnicianProfile(data) {
          return data.get('suspended', false) != true &&
            (
              data.get('status', '') in ['active', 'ACTIVE', 'approved', 'APPROVED'] ||
              data.get('approvalStatus', '') in ['approved', 'APPROVED']
            );
        }
    
        function hasApprovedTechnicianRecord() {
          // Technician writes require the dedicated operational profile. The users
          // document remains an identity/cache record and is not an approval source.
          return signedIn() &&
            exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/technicians/$(request.auth.uid)) &&
            approvedTechnicianProfile(get(/databases/$(database)/documents/technicians/$(request.auth.uid)).data);
        }
    
        function isApprovedTechnician() {
          return hasTechnicianClaim() && (
            (
              exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/technicians/$(request.auth.uid)) &&
              approvedTechnicianProfile(get(/databases/$(database)/documents/technicians/$(request.auth.uid)).data)
            ) ||
            (
              exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)) &&
              approvedTechnicianProfile(get(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)).data)
            )
          );
        }
    
        function isTechnicianActor() {
          return isApprovedTechnician();
        }
    
        function hasTechnicianDispatchAuthority() {
          return canDispatchJobs();
        }
    
        function participantCanRead(data) { return isAdmin() || ownerCanRead(data) || tenantOwns(data) || (isApprovedTechnician() && techOwns(data)) || brokerOwns(data); }
    
        function staffRequestCreate(data) {
          return staffCanCreate(data) &&
            (!('status' in data) || data.status in ['pending_hr_review', 'pending_finance_review', 'submitted']) &&
            (!('paperless' in data) || data.paperless == true) &&
            (!('payrollApproved' in data) || data.payrollApproved == false) &&
            (!('hrApproved' in data) || data.hrApproved == false) &&
            (!('approved' in data) || data.approved == false);
        }
    
        function ownerDraftCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() && owns(data) &&
            (!('status' in data) || data.status in ['draft', 'quote_generated', 'contract_selected', 'payment_pending', 'pending_admin_approval', 'pending_approval']) &&
            (!('paymentVerified' in data) || data.paymentVerified == false) &&
            (!('adminApproved' in data) || data.adminApproved == false) &&
            (!('approved' in data) || data.approved == false) &&
            (!('contractActivated' in data) || data.contractActivated == false) &&
            (!('unlocksDashboard' in data) || data.unlocksDashboard == false);
        }
    
        function ownerContractDraftCreate(data) {
          return ownerDraftCreate(data) &&
            !data.keys().hasAny([
              'quoteHash',
              'quoteSnapshot',
              'quoteVersion',
              'otpVerificationId',
              'ownerSigned',
              'signatureStatus',
              'signatureState',
              'paymentReferenceId',
              'paymentProofUrl',
              'paymentProofPath'
            ]);
        }
    
        function paymentConfirmationCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() && tenantOwns(data) &&
            (!('status' in data) || data.status in ['pending_verification', 'pending']) &&
            (!('paymentVerified' in data) || data.paymentVerified == false) &&
            (!('approved' in data) || data.approved == false);
        }
    
        function safeClientNotificationCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            (('recipientId' in data && data.recipientId == request.auth.uid) || ('userId' in data && data.userId == request.auth.uid)) &&
            (!('recipientId' in data) || data.recipientId == request.auth.uid) &&
            (!('userId' in data) || data.userId == request.auth.uid) &&
            (!('createdBy' in data) || data.createdBy == request.auth.uid) &&
            data.type in ['USER_NOTE', 'PROFILE_REMINDER', 'PREFERENCE_UPDATE', 'SUPPORT_RECEIPT'] &&
            data.title is string && data.title.size() > 0 && data.title.size() <= 120 &&
            data.body is string && data.body.size() > 0 && data.body.size() <= 1000 &&
            (!('read' in data) || data.read == false) &&
            (!('priority' in data) || data.priority in ['LOW', 'NORMAL']) &&
            request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly(['recipientId', 'userId', 'createdBy', 'type', 'title', 'body', 'read', 'priority', 'link', 'createdAt']);
        }
    
        function safeNotificationReadUpdate() {
          return signedIn() &&
            (
              resource.data.get('recipientId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
              resource.data.get('userId', null) == request.auth.uid
            ) &&
            request.resource.data.get('recipientId', null) == resource.data.get('recipientId', null) &&
            request.resource.data.get('userId', null) == resource.data.get('userId', null) &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['read', 'readAt']) &&
            request.resource.data.get('read', false) == true;
        }
    
    
    
        function safeTenantUnitLinkRequestCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            tenantOwns(data) &&
            data.status == 'PENDING_ADMIN_REVIEW' &&
            data.verificationState == 'ADMIN_OR_OWNER_VERIFICATION_REQUIRED' &&
            data.unitNumber is string &&
            data.unitNumber.size() > 0 &&
            (!('approvedAt' in data) || data.approvedAt == null) &&
            (!('approvedBy' in data) || data.approvedBy == null) &&
            (!('linkedUnitId' in data) || data.linkedUnitId == null) &&
            (!('linkedAt' in data) || data.linkedAt == null);
        }
    
        function safeOwnerContractUpdate() {
          return signedIn() && owns(resource.data) &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'acceptedAt', 'signedAt', 'updatedAt']) &&
            (!('paymentVerified' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.paymentVerified == resource.data.get('paymentVerified', null)) &&
            (!('status' in request.resource.data) || !(request.resource.data.status in ['active', 'ACTIVE']));
        }
    
        function safeOwnerPropertyUpdate() {
          return signedIn() &&
            owns(resource.data) &&
            request.resource.data.get('ownerId', null) == resource.data.get('ownerId', null) &&
            request.resource.data.get('ownerUid', null) == resource.data.get('ownerUid', null) &&
            !request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasAny([
              'status',
              'activationStatus',
              'paymentStatus',
              'paymentVerified',
              'adminApproved',
              'approved',
              'contractActivated',

              'quoteHash',

              'quoteVersion',
              'ownerId',
              'ownerUid'
            ]);
        }
    
        function safeTenantEvidenceUpdate() {
          return signedIn() &&
            // Reject unrelated mutations before database reads and array invariants.
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'photos',
              'primaryPhotoUrl',
              'tenantPhotos',
              'evidenceStatus',
              'evidenceUploadedAt',
              'evidenceUploadError',
              'updatedAt'
            ]) &&
            isNotSuspended() &&
            resource.data.get('evidenceStatus', '') != 'TENANT_EVIDENCE_UPLOADED' &&
            (!('evidenceStatus' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.evidenceStatus in ['PENDING_TENANT_UPLOAD', 'TENANT_EVIDENCE_UPLOADED', 'TENANT_EVIDENCE_UPLOAD_FAILED']) &&
            request.resource.data.get('photos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('photos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('photos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('photos', [])) &&
            request.resource.data.get('tenantPhotos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('tenantPhotos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('tenantPhotos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('tenantPhotos', [])) &&
            (
              resource.data.get('primaryPhotoUrl', '') == '' ||
              request.resource.data.get('primaryPhotoUrl', '') == resource.data.get('primaryPhotoUrl', '')
            );
        }
    
    
    
        function canCreateTenantBoundTicket(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            claimedRole() == 'tenant' &&
            tenantOwns(data) &&
            isNotSuspended() &&
            data.keys().hasAll(['unitId', 'propertyId']) &&
            data.get('unitId', null) is string &&
            data.get('propertyId', null) is string &&
            data.get('unitId', '').size() > 0 &&
            isUnitTenant(data.get('unitId', null)) &&
            data.get('propertyId', null) == get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(data.get('unitId', null))).data.get('propertyId', null) &&
            (!('ownerId' in data) || data.get('ownerId', null) == get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(data.get('unitId', null))).data.get('ownerId', null)) &&
            (!('ownerUid' in data) || data.get('ownerUid', null) == get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(data.get('unitId', null))).data.get('ownerUid', null)) &&
            data.get('source', '') == 'TENANT_PORTAL' &&
            data.get('status', '') == 'OPEN' &&
            data.get('evidenceStatus', '') == 'PENDING_TENANT_UPLOAD' &&
            data.get('assignedTechnicianId', null) == null &&
            data.get('technicianId', null) == null &&
            !data.keys().hasAny([
              'paymentStatus',
              'paymentVerified',
              'adminApproved',
              'approved',
              'estimatedCost',
              'slaCredit',
              'completedAt',
              'closedAt',
              'acceptedAt',
              'arrivedAt',
              'startedAt'
            ]);
        }
    
        function safeDispatcherTicketUpdate() {
          // Non-dispatch callers and unrelated mutations fail before database reads.
          return hasNonAdminDispatchClaimOnly() &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'assignedTechnicianId',
              'technicianId',
              'techId',
              'status',
              'dispatchStatus',
              'assignedAt',
              'autoAssignedAt',
              'reassignedAt',
              'assignmentSource',
              'reassignmentReason',
              'assignedBy',
              'assignedByRole',
              'updatedAt'
            ]) &&
            isNotSuspended() &&
            request.resource.data.get('status', resource.data.get('status', '')) in [
              'OPEN',
              'open',
              'PENDING_ASSIGNMENT',
              'pending_assignment',
              'EMERGENCY_SUBMITTED',
              'emergency_submitted',
              'ASSIGNED',
              'assigned',
              'AUTO_ASSIGNED',
              'auto_assigned',
              'REASSIGNED',
              'reassigned'
            ];
        }
    
        function safeTechnicianTicketUpdate() {
          // The outer allow rule already verifies the technician claim and current
          // assignment. Keep this helper bounded to mutable evidence fields,
          // suspension, dedicated-profile approval, final-state protection, and
          // append-only completion proof.
          return request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'updatedAt',
              'technicianNotes',
              'techNotes',
              'workNotes',
              'notes',
              'afterPhotoUrl',
              'proofPhotos',
              'completionPhotos',
              'evidencePhotos',
              'evidenceStatus',
              'resolutionSummary',
              'materialsUsed',
              'partsDisposition',
              'proofReadiness',
              'arrivedLocation',
              'technicianLocation',
              'technicianLocationUpdatedAt'
            ]) &&
            isNotSuspended() &&
            hasApprovedTechnicianRecord() &&
            !(resource.data.get('status', '') in ['COMPLETED', 'completed', 'CLOSED', 'closed', 'TENANT_APPROVED']) &&
            request.resource.data.get('proofPhotos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('proofPhotos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('proofPhotos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('proofPhotos', [])) &&
            request.resource.data.get('completionPhotos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('completionPhotos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('completionPhotos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('completionPhotos', [])) &&
            request.resource.data.get('evidencePhotos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('evidencePhotos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('evidencePhotos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('evidencePhotos', [])) &&
            (
              resource.data.get('afterPhotoUrl', '') == '' ||
              request.resource.data.get('afterPhotoUrl', '') == resource.data.get('afterPhotoUrl', '')
            );
        }

        // Direct client open-mission claims are denied. Assignment requires

            (!('propertyId' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.propertyId == resource.data.get('propertyId', null)) &&
            (!('unitId' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.unitId == resource.data.get('unitId', null));
        }
    
        function safeOwnerProfileCreate(data, ownerId) {
          return signedIn() && request.auth.uid == ownerId &&
            (!('role' in data) || data.role == 'owner') &&
            (!('status' in data) || !(data.status in ['ACTIVE','active'])) &&
            (!('dashboardUnlocked' in data)) &&
            (!('paymentVerified' in data));
        }
    
        function safeOwnerProfileUpdate() {
          return signedIn() &&
            (!('status' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.status == resource.data.get('status', null)) &&
            (!('dashboardUnlocked' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.dashboardUnlocked == resource.data.get('dashboardUnlocked', null)) &&
            (!('dashboardLocked' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.dashboardLocked == resource.data.get('dashboardLocked', null)) &&
            (!('dashboardUnlockApproved' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.dashboardUnlockApproved == resource.data.get('dashboardUnlockApproved', null)) &&
            (!('paymentVerified' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.paymentVerified == resource.data.get('paymentVerified', null)) &&
            (!('paymentStatus' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.paymentStatus == resource.data.get('paymentStatus', null)) &&
            (!('adminApproved' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.adminApproved == resource.data.get('adminApproved', null)) &&
            (!('approved' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.approved == resource.data.get('approved', null)) &&
            (!('activeContractId' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.activeContractId == resource.data.get('activeContractId', null)) &&
            (!('activationStatus' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.activationStatus == resource.data.get('activationStatus', null)) &&
            (!('contractActivated' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.contractActivated == resource.data.get('contractActivated', null));
        }
    
        function ownsOnboardingProof(data) {
          return signedIn() && (
            data.get('ownerUid', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('ownerId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('userId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('createdByUid', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            emailMatches(data.get('email', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('ownerEmail', null))
          );
        }
    
        function safeOnboardingProofCreate(data) {
          return ownsOnboardingProof(data) &&
            (!('verified' in data) || data.verified == false) &&
            (!('approved' in data) || data.approved == false) &&
            (!('adminApproved' in data) || data.adminApproved == false) &&
            (!('paymentVerified' in data) || data.paymentVerified == false);
        }
    
        // ── AUDIT LOGS — server-only writes ──────────────────────────────────────
        // Client code MUST NOT write directly to audit_logs or auditLogs.
        // All writes must go through the logUserAuditAction Cloud Function which
        // uses the Admin SDK and therefore bypasses these rules entirely.
        match /audit_logs/{logId} {
          allow read: if isAdmin() || isAuditor();
          allow create: if false;  // server-only via Cloud Function
          allow update: if false;
          allow delete: if false;
        }
        match /auditLogs/{logId} {
          allow read: if isAdmin() || isAuditor();
          allow create: if false;  // server-only via Cloud Function
          allow update: if false;
          allow delete: if false;
        }
    
        match /system_health/{healthDoc} {
          allow read: if isAdmin();
          allow write: if false;  // server-only via Admin SDK launch evidence jobs
        }
    
        match /users/{userId} {
          allow get: if signedIn() && (
                        (request.auth.uid == userId && (resource == null || resource.data.get('suspended', false) != true)) ||
                        (
                          request.auth.uid != userId &&
                          request.auth.token.get('suspended', false) != true &&
                          (
                            (resource.data.get('role', '') == 'tenant' &&
                             ((resource.data.get('ownerId', '') != '' && resource.data.get('ownerId', '') == request.auth.uid) ||
                              emailMatchesCycleFree(resource.data.get('ownerEmail', null)))) ||
                            emailOwnsCycleFree(resource.data) ||
                            canReadUserDirectoryCycleFree()
                          )
                        )
                      );
          allow list: if (request.auth != null && request.auth.token.get('suspended', false) != true) && (
                        canReadUserDirectoryCycleFree() ||
                        (signedIn() &&
                         resource.data.get('role', '') == 'tenant' &&
                         resource.data.get('ownerId', null) == request.auth.uid)
                      );
          allow create: if isAdmin() || safeUserBootstrapCreate(request.resource.data, userId);
          allow update: if isNotSuspended() && (isAdmin() || safeUserSelfUpdate(userId));
          allow delete: if isNotSuspended() && isAdmin();
          match /fcmTokens/{tokenId} {
            allow read: if isNotSuspended() &&
              (request.auth.uid == userId || isAdmin() || isHr());
            allow create, update, delete: if isNotSuspended() &&
              (request.auth.uid == userId || isAdmin());
          }
    
          match /deviceReadiness/{readinessId} {
            allow read: if isNotSuspended() &&
              (request.auth.uid == userId || isAdmin() || isHr());
            allow create, update, delete: if isNotSuspended() &&
              (request.auth.uid == userId || isAdmin());
          }
    
          // Unknown and future user subcollections are denied until explicitly
          // reviewed and allowlisted above.
          match /{subcollection}/{document=**} {
            allow read, write: if false;
          }
        }
    
        match /owners/{ownerId} {
          allow read: if isOwner(ownerId) || emailOwns(resource.data) || isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageTenants');
          allow create: if isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageTenants') || safeOwnerProfileCreate(request.resource.data, ownerId);
          allow update: if isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageTenants') || (isOwner(ownerId) && safeOwnerProfileUpdate());
          allow delete: if isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageTenants');
        }
    
        match /tenants/{tenantId} {
          allow read: if (signedIn() && request.auth.uid == tenantId) || isAdmin() || participantCanRead(resource.data);
          allow create: if isAdmin();
          allow update: if isAdmin();
          allow delete: if isAdmin();
        }
    
        match /technicians/{techId} {
          allow read: if (signedIn() && request.auth.uid == techId) || isAdmin() || isHr() || isOps() || hasPermission('canManageTechnicians') || emailOwns(resource.data);
          allow create: if isAdmin() || isHr() || isOps() || hasPermission('canManageTechnicians');
          allow update: if isAdmin() || isHr() || isOps() || hasPermission('canManageTechnicians') || safeTechnicianProfileUpdate(techId);
          allow delete: if isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageTechnicians');
    
          match /deviceReadiness/{readinessId} {
            allow read: if isAdmin() || isOps() || (signedIn() && request.auth.uid == techId);
            allow create, update: if isAdmin() || isOps() || (isApprovedTechnician() && request.auth.uid == techId);
            allow delete: if isAdmin() || isOps();
          }
        }
    
        match /properties/{propertyId} {
          allow get: if isNotSuspended() && getTenantPropertyId() == propertyId;
          allow read: if isNotSuspended() && (canManageProperties() || ownerCanRead(resource.data) || tenantOwns(resource.data) || (isTechnicianActor() && techOwns(resource.data)));
          allow create: if isNotSuspended() && (canManageProperties() || ownerDraftCreate(request.resource.data));
          allow update: if isNotSuspended() && (canManageProperties() || safeOwnerPropertyUpdate());
          allow delete: if isNotSuspended() && isAdmin();
        }
    
        match /units/{unitId} {
          allow get: if signedIn() && (resource.data.get('tenantId', null) == request.auth.uid || resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid || resource.data.get('userId', null) == request.auth.uid || emailMatches(resource.data.get('tenantEmail', null)));
          allow list: if signedIn() && resource.data.tenantId == request.auth.uid;
          allow list: if signedIn() && resource.data.tenantUid == request.auth.uid;
          allow list: if emailMatches(resource.data.get('tenantEmail', null));
          allow read: if canManageProperties() || ownerCanRead(resource.data) || tenantOwns(resource.data) || emailOwns(resource.data);
          allow create: if canManageProperties();
          allow update: if canManageProperties();
          allow delete: if canManageProperties();
        }
    
        match /tenant_unit_link_requests/{requestId} {

          allow create: if isAdmin() || safeTenantUnitLinkRequestCreate(request.resource.data);
          allow update: if isAdmin();
          allow delete: if isAdmin();
        }
    
        match /contracts/{contractId} {
          allow read: if participantCanRead(resource.data) || emailOwns(resource.data) || canManageContracts();

        }
    
        match /payments/{paymentId} {
          allow read: if participantCanRead(resource.data) || (signedIn() && resource.data.get('payerId', null) == request.auth.uid) || canManageContracts();
          allow create, update, delete: if false;
        }
    
        match /payment_transactions/{paymentId} {
          allow read: if participantCanRead(resource.data) || (signedIn() && resource.data.get('payerId', null) == request.auth.uid) || canManageContracts();
          // Financial evidence is always created by a validated Cloud Function.
          // A pending client-authored row could otherwise choose a privileged
          // transaction type and enter a weaker admin approval path.
          allow create: if false;
          allow update, delete: if false;
        }
    
        match /paymentConfirmations/{confirmationId} {
          allow read: if canManageContracts() || tenantOwns(resource.data);
          allow create: if canManageContracts() || paymentConfirmationCreate(request.resource.data);
          allow update: if canManageContracts();
          allow delete: if isAdmin();
        }
    
        match /tickets/{ticketId} {
          allow read: if isNotSuspended() && (participantCanRead(resource.data) || canDispatchJobs());
          allow create: if isAdmin() || canCreateTenantBoundTicket(request.resource.data);
          allow update: if isAdmin() && isNotSuspended();
          allow update: if hasNonAdminDispatchClaimOnly() && safeDispatcherTicketUpdate();
          allow update: if tenantOwns(resource.data) && safeTenantEvidenceUpdate();
          allow update: if hasTechnicianClaim() && techOwns(resource.data) && safeTechnicianTicketUpdate();
          allow delete: if isAdmin();
        }
    
        match /maintenanceTickets/{ticketId} {
          allow read: if isNotSuspended() && (participantCanRead(resource.data) || canDispatchJobs());
          allow create: if isAdmin() || canCreateTenantBoundTicket(request.resource.data);
          allow update: if isAdmin() && isNotSuspended();
          allow update: if hasNonAdminDispatchClaimOnly() && safeDispatcherTicketUpdate();
          allow update: if tenantOwns(resource.data) && safeTenantEvidenceUpdate();
          allow update: if hasTechnicianClaim() && techOwns(resource.data) && safeTechnicianTicketUpdate();
          allow delete: if isAdmin();
          match /messages/{messageId} {
            allow read: if canAccessMaintenanceTicket(ticketId);
            allow create: if safeTicketMessageCreate(ticketId);
            allow update: if false;
            allow delete: if isAdmin();
          }
        }
    
        match /documents/{documentId} {

          allow delete: if canManageProperties();
        }
    
        match /intake_submissions/{intakeId} {

        match /systemLogs/{logId} {

          allow update: if isConfidentialRequest(resource.data) ? isHrManagerTier() : (isHr() || isFinance() || isOps());
          allow delete: if isHr() || isAdmin();
        }
    
        match /hrAiConversations/{caseId} {
          allow read: if isConfidentialRequest(resource.data)

            : (isHr() || staffCanRead(resource.data));
          allow create: if isHr() || staffRequestCreate(request.resource.data);
          allow update: if isConfidentialRequest(resource.data) ? isHrManagerTier() : isHr();
          allow delete: if isHr();
        }

        match /gatePasses/{passId} {

        match /amenities/{amenityId} {
          allow read: if propertyScopedRead(resource.data);
          allow create: if propertyScopedOwnerWrite(request.resource.data);
          allow update: if propertyScopedOwnerWrite(resource.data) && docPropertyId(request.resource.data) == docPropertyId(resource.data);
          allow delete: if propertyScopedOwnerWrite(resource.data);

        match /amenityBookings/{bookingId} {
          allow read: if isAdmin() || (docPropertyId(resource.data) != null && isPropertyOwner(docPropertyId(resource.data))) || tenantUidOwns(resource.data);
          allow create: if isAdmin() || (signedIn() && request.resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid && getTenantPropertyId() == docPropertyId(request.resource.data));
          allow update: if isAdmin() || (docPropertyId(resource.data) != null && isPropertyOwner(docPropertyId(resource.data))) || (signedIn() && tenantUidOwns(resource.data) && request.resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid && docPropertyId(resource.data) != null && getTenantPropertyId() == docPropertyId(resource.data));
          allow delete: if isAdmin() || (docPropertyId(resource.data) != null && isPropertyOwner(docPropertyId(resource.data))) || tenantUidOwns(resource.data);

        match /announcements/{announcementId} {
          allow read: if propertyScopedRead(resource.data);
          allow create: if propertyScopedOwnerWrite(request.resource.data);
          allow update: if propertyScopedOwnerWrite(resource.data) && docPropertyId(request.resource.data) == docPropertyId(resource.data);
          allow delete: if propertyScopedOwnerWrite(resource.data);
        }
    
        match /announcementReads/{readId} {
          allow read: if isAdmin() || (
            signedIn() &&
            resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            resource.data.get('propertyId', null) == getTenantPropertyId()
          );
          allow create: if signedIn() &&
            request.resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            request.resource.data.get('propertyId', null) == getTenantPropertyId() &&
            request.resource.data.get('announcementId', null) is string &&
            request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly(['announcementId', 'tenantUid', 'propertyId', 'readAt']);
          allow update: if signedIn() &&
            resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            request.resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            request.resource.data.get('propertyId', null) == resource.data.get('propertyId', null) &&
            request.resource.data.get('announcementId', null) == resource.data.get('announcementId', null) &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['readAt']);
          allow delete: if isAdmin() || (signedIn() && resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid);
        }
    
        // Deterministic-id slot locks stay property scoped; clients must query by
        // propertyId and cannot enumerate another building's tenant/time records.
        match /amenitySlots/{slotId} {
          allow read: if propertyScopedRead(resource.data);
          allow create: if signedIn() &&
            request.resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            request.resource.data.get('propertyId', null) is string &&
            request.resource.data.get('propertyId', '').size() > 0 &&
            getTenantPropertyId() != null &&
            request.resource.data.get('propertyId', null) == getTenantPropertyId();
          allow delete: if isAdmin() || tenantUidOwns(resource.data);
        }
    
        match /tenantDocuments/{documentId} {
          allow read: if isAdmin() || (signedIn() && (
            resource.data.get('tenantId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            resource.data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            resource.data.get('tenantId', null) == 'ALL' ||
            emailMatches(resource.data.get('tenantEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(resource.data.get('recipientEmail', null))
          ));
          allow create: if isAdmin();
          allow update: if isAdmin();
          allow delete: if isAdmin();
        }
    
        match /documentLibrary/{documentId} {
          allow read: if propertyScopedRead(resource.data);

        function isBinConnectParticipant(data) {

            isAdmin() ||
            (data.get('participantIds', []) is list && request.auth.uid in data.get('participantIds', [])) ||

        function safeBinConnectThreadCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            data.get('createdBy', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            data.get('participantIds', []) is list &&
            data.get('participantIds', []).size() == 1 &&
            data.get('participantIds', [])[0] == request.auth.uid &&
            (!('assignedAdminId' in data) || data.get('assignedAdminId', null) == null) &&
            data.get('status', null) in ['open', 'pending', 'new'] &&
            data.get('channel', null) in ['company_ceo', 'admin_support', 'owner_to_tenant', 'owner_to_technician', 'majlis_staff', 'maintenance_chat', 'feature_suggestion', 'dashboard_issue'];
        }

    rules_version = '2';
    service cloud.firestore {
      match /databases/{database}/documents {
        function signedIn() {
          // Suspension is an Auth control: admin suspension callables disable the
          // user, set this claim, and revoke refresh tokens. Reading users/{uid}
          // inside this foundational helper multiplies across nested predicates
          // and can exceed Firestore's 1,000-expression evaluation limit.
          return request.auth != null &&
            request.auth.token.get('suspended', false) != true;
        }
    
        function claimedRole() {
          return signedIn()
            ? request.auth.token.get('role', request.auth.token.get('userRole', request.auth.token.get('primaryRole', '')))
            : '';
        }
    
        function isNotSuspended() {
          return signedIn() && (
            !exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)) ||
            get(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)).data.get('suspended', false) != true
          );
        }
    
        function hasAdminClaimCycleFree() {
          return signedIn() && (
            (
              claimedRole() == '' &&
              (
                ('admin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('admin', false) == true) ||
                ('isAdmin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('isAdmin', false) == true)

            ('ceo' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('ceo', false) == true) ||

          );
        }
    
        function canReadUserDirectoryCycleFree() {
          return signedIn() && (
            hasAdminClaimCycleFree() || (
              signedIn() &&

                'account_manager',
                'operations_admin',
                'operations_manager',
                'dispatcher'
              ]
            )
          );
        }
    
        function emailMatchesCycleFree(value) {
          return signedIn() &&
            request.auth.token.get('email_verified', false) == true &&
            authEmail() != null &&
            value != null &&
            value == authEmail();
        }
    
        function emailOwnsCycleFree(data) {
          return emailMatchesCycleFree(data.get('email', null)) ||
            emailMatchesCycleFree(data.get('ownerEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatchesCycleFree(data.get('tenantEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatchesCycleFree(data.get('technicianEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatchesCycleFree(data.get('staffEmail', null)) ||

            emailMatchesCycleFree(data.get('recipientEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatchesCycleFree(data.get('contactEmail', null));
        }
    
        function hasAdminClaim() {
          return signedIn() && (
            (
              claimedRole() == '' &&
              (
                ('admin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('admin', false) == true) ||
                ('isAdmin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('isAdmin', false) == true)
              )
            ) ||
            ('superAdmin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('superAdmin', false) == true) ||
            ('super_admin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.get('super_admin', false) == true) ||

          );
        }
    
        function isAdmin() {
          // Firestore profile fields are display/cache data, never an authorization
          // source. Privileged access must be backed by signed Auth custom claims.
          return hasAdminClaim();
        }
    
        function isHr() {
          return signedIn() && (isAdmin() || (signedIn() && (

          )));
        }

            ('userRole' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.userRole in ['hr_admin', 'hr_manager']) ||
            ('primaryRole' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.primaryRole in ['hr_admin', 'hr_manager'])
          )));

          )));

        function isAuditor() {
          return signedIn() && (isAdmin() || (signedIn() && (

        function hasPermission(permission) {
          return signedIn() && 'permissions' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.permissions.get(permission, false) == true;
        }
    
        function canManageProperties() { return isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageProperties'); }
        function canManageContracts() { return isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageContracts'); }
        function canManageCompanyProfile() { return isAdmin() || hasPermission('canManageCompanyProfile'); }
        function canReadUserDirectory() {

                'hr_manager',

                'operations_manager',
                'dispatcher'
              ]
            )
          );
        }
        function hasDispatchAuthorityClaimOnly() {
          return signedIn() && (
            hasAdminClaim() ||
            hasPermission('canDispatchJobs') ||
            claimedRole() in ['operations_admin', 'operations_manager', 'dispatcher']
          );
        }
    
        function hasNonAdminDispatchClaimOnly() {
          return signedIn() && (
            hasPermission('canDispatchJobs') ||
            claimedRole() in ['operations_admin', 'operations_manager', 'dispatcher']
          );
        }
    
        function canDispatchJobs() {
          // Check inexpensive signed claims first. The database-backed suspension
          // lookup runs only for callers that actually possess dispatch authority.
          return hasDispatchAuthorityClaimOnly() && isNotSuspended();
        }
    
        function authEmail() { return signedIn() ? request.auth.token.get('email', null) : null; }
        function emailMatches(value) {
          return signedIn() &&
            request.auth.token.get('email_verified', false) == true &&
            authEmail() != null &&
            value != null &&
            value == authEmail();
        }
        function isOwnerId(ownerId) { return signedIn() && ownerId != null && ownerId == request.auth.uid; }
        function isOwner(ownerId) { return isOwnerId(ownerId); }
        function isTenantId(tenantId) { return signedIn() && tenantId != null && tenantId == request.auth.uid; }
        function isTechnicianId(techId) { return signedIn() && techId != null && techId == request.auth.uid; }
    
        function getTenantPropertyId() {
          return exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)) ? get(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)).data.get('propertyId', null) : null;
        }
        function isUnitTenant(unitId) {
          return signedIn() &&
            unitId is string &&
            exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(unitId)) && (
              get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(unitId)).data.get('tenantId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
              get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(unitId)).data.get('tenantUid', null) == request.auth.uid ||
              get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(unitId)).data.get('userId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
              get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(unitId)).data.get('authUid', null) == request.auth.uid ||
              emailMatches(get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(unitId)).data.get('tenantEmail', null))
            );
        }
        function isPropertyOwner(propertyId) {
          return signedIn() &&
            exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/properties/$(propertyId)) && (
              get(/databases/$(database)/documents/properties/$(propertyId)).data.get('ownerId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
              get(/databases/$(database)/documents/properties/$(propertyId)).data.get('ownerUid', null) == request.auth.uid
            );
        }
    
        function owns(data) {
          return isOwnerId(data.get('ownerId', null)) || isOwnerId(data.get('ownerUid', null)) || isOwnerId(data.get('authUid', null)) || isOwnerId(data.get('userId', null)) || isOwnerId(data.get('createdBy', null)) || isOwnerId(data.get('createdByUid', null));
        }
    
        function tenantOwns(data) {
          return isTenantId(data.get('tenantId', null)) || isTenantId(data.get('tenantUid', null)) || isTenantId(data.get('authUid', null)) || isTenantId(data.get('userId', null)) || isTenantId(data.get('createdByUid', null));
        }
    
        function techOwns(data) {
          return isTechnicianId(data.get('assignedTechnicianId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('technicianId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('techId', null));
        }
    
        function brokerOwns(data) {
          return signedIn() && (
            data.get('brokerId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('brokerUid', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('userId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('createdByUid', null) == request.auth.uid
          );
        }
    
        function emailOwns(data) {
          return emailMatches(data.get('email', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('ownerEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('tenantEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('technicianEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('staffEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('brokerEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('recipientEmail', null)) ||
            emailMatches(data.get('contactEmail', null));
        }
    
        function ownerCanRead(data) { return signedIn() && (owns(data) || emailOwns(data)); }
        function staffCanRead(data) { return signedIn() && (techOwns(data) || isTechnicianId(data.get('uid', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('userId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('technicianId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('staffId', null)) || emailOwns(data)); }
        function staffCanCreate(data) { return signedIn() && (isTechnicianId(data.get('uid', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('userId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('technicianId', null)) || isTechnicianId(data.get('staffId', null)) || emailOwns(data)); }
        function docPropertyId(data) { return data.get('propertyId', null); }
        function docUnitId(data) { return data.get('unitId', null); }
        function tenantUidOwns(data) { return isTenantId(data.get('tenantUid', null)); }
        function propertyScopedRead(data) {
          return signedIn() && (
            isAdmin() ||
            (docPropertyId(data) != null && isPropertyOwner(docPropertyId(data))) ||
            (docPropertyId(data) != null && getTenantPropertyId() == docPropertyId(data))
          );
        }
        function propertyScopedOwnerWrite(data) {
          return isAdmin() || (docPropertyId(data) != null && isPropertyOwner(docPropertyId(data)));
        }
        function isConfidentialRequest(data) {
          return ('requestType' in data && data.requestType == 'manager_issue') || ('category' in data && data.category == 'confidential');
        }
    
        function hasTechnicianClaim() {
          return signedIn() && (
            ('role' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.role == 'technician') ||
            ('userRole' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.userRole == 'technician') ||
            ('primaryRole' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.primaryRole == 'technician')
          );
        }
    
        function approvedTechnicianProfile(data) {
          return data.get('suspended', false) != true &&
            (
              data.get('status', '') in ['active', 'ACTIVE', 'approved', 'APPROVED'] ||
              data.get('approvalStatus', '') in ['approved', 'APPROVED']
            );
        }
    
        function hasApprovedTechnicianRecord() {
          // Technician writes require the dedicated operational profile. The users
          // document remains an identity/cache record and is not an approval source.
          return signedIn() &&
            exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/technicians/$(request.auth.uid)) &&
            approvedTechnicianProfile(get(/databases/$(database)/documents/technicians/$(request.auth.uid)).data);
        }
    
        function isApprovedTechnician() {
          return hasTechnicianClaim() && (
            (
              exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/technicians/$(request.auth.uid)) &&
              approvedTechnicianProfile(get(/databases/$(database)/documents/technicians/$(request.auth.uid)).data)
            ) ||
            (
              exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)) &&
              approvedTechnicianProfile(get(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)).data)
            )
          );
        }
    
        function isTechnicianActor() {
          return isApprovedTechnician();
        }
    
        function hasTechnicianDispatchAuthority() {
          return canDispatchJobs();
        }
    
        function participantCanRead(data) { return isAdmin() || ownerCanRead(data) || tenantOwns(data) || (isApprovedTechnician() && techOwns(data)) || brokerOwns(data); }
    
        function staffRequestCreate(data) {
          return staffCanCreate(data) &&
            (!('status' in data) || data.status in ['pending_hr_review', 'pending_finance_review', 'submitted']) &&
            (!('paperless' in data) || data.paperless == true) &&
            (!('payrollApproved' in data) || data.payrollApproved == false) &&
            (!('hrApproved' in data) || data.hrApproved == false) &&
            (!('approved' in data) || data.approved == false);
        }
    
        function ownerDraftCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() && owns(data) &&
            (!('status' in data) || data.status in ['draft', 'quote_generated', 'contract_selected', 'payment_pending', 'pending_admin_approval', 'pending_approval']) &&
            (!('paymentVerified' in data) || data.paymentVerified == false) &&
            (!('adminApproved' in data) || data.adminApproved == false) &&
            (!('approved' in data) || data.approved == false) &&
            (!('contractActivated' in data) || data.contractActivated == false) &&
            (!('unlocksDashboard' in data) || data.unlocksDashboard == false);
        }
    
        function ownerContractDraftCreate(data) {
          return ownerDraftCreate(data) &&
            !data.keys().hasAny([
              'quoteHash',
              'quoteSnapshot',
              'quoteVersion',
              'otpVerificationId',
              'ownerSigned',
              'signatureStatus',
              'signatureState',
              'paymentReferenceId',
              'paymentProofUrl',
              'paymentProofPath'
            ]);
        }
    
        function paymentConfirmationCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() && tenantOwns(data) &&
            (!('status' in data) || data.status in ['pending_verification', 'pending']) &&
            (!('paymentVerified' in data) || data.paymentVerified == false) &&
            (!('approved' in data) || data.approved == false);
        }
    
        function safeClientNotificationCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            (('recipientId' in data && data.recipientId == request.auth.uid) || ('userId' in data && data.userId == request.auth.uid)) &&
            (!('recipientId' in data) || data.recipientId == request.auth.uid) &&
            (!('userId' in data) || data.userId == request.auth.uid) &&
            (!('createdBy' in data) || data.createdBy == request.auth.uid) &&
            data.type in ['USER_NOTE', 'PROFILE_REMINDER', 'PREFERENCE_UPDATE', 'SUPPORT_RECEIPT'] &&
            data.title is string && data.title.size() > 0 && data.title.size() <= 120 &&
            data.body is string && data.body.size() > 0 && data.body.size() <= 1000 &&
            (!('read' in data) || data.read == false) &&
            (!('priority' in data) || data.priority in ['LOW', 'NORMAL']) &&
            request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly(['recipientId', 'userId', 'createdBy', 'type', 'title', 'body', 'read', 'priority', 'link', 'createdAt']);
        }
    
        function safeNotificationReadUpdate() {
          return signedIn() &&
            (
              resource.data.get('recipientId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
              resource.data.get('userId', null) == request.auth.uid
            ) &&
            request.resource.data.get('recipientId', null) == resource.data.get('recipientId', null) &&
            request.resource.data.get('userId', null) == resource.data.get('userId', null) &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['read', 'readAt']) &&
            request.resource.data.get('read', false) == true;
        }
    
    
    
        function safeTenantUnitLinkRequestCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            tenantOwns(data) &&
            data.status == 'PENDING_ADMIN_REVIEW' &&
            data.verificationState == 'ADMIN_OR_OWNER_VERIFICATION_REQUIRED' &&
            data.unitNumber is string &&
            data.unitNumber.size() > 0 &&
            (!('approvedAt' in data) || data.approvedAt == null) &&
            (!('approvedBy' in data) || data.approvedBy == null) &&
            (!('linkedUnitId' in data) || data.linkedUnitId == null) &&
            (!('linkedAt' in data) || data.linkedAt == null);
        }
    
        function safeOwnerContractUpdate() {
          return signedIn() && owns(resource.data) &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'acceptedAt', 'signedAt', 'updatedAt']) &&
            (!('paymentVerified' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.paymentVerified == resource.data.get('paymentVerified', null)) &&
            (!('status' in request.resource.data) || !(request.resource.data.status in ['active', 'ACTIVE']));
        }
    
        function safeOwnerPropertyUpdate() {
          return signedIn() &&
            owns(resource.data) &&
            request.resource.data.get('ownerId', null) == resource.data.get('ownerId', null) &&
            request.resource.data.get('ownerUid', null) == resource.data.get('ownerUid', null) &&
            !request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasAny([
              'status',
              'activationStatus',
              'paymentStatus',
              'paymentVerified',
              'adminApproved',
              'approved',
              'contractActivated',
              'dashboardUnlocked',
              'dashboardUnlockApproved',
              'unlocksDashboard',
              'activeContractId',
              'quoteHash',
              'quoteSnapshot',
              'quoteVersion',
              'ownerId',
              'ownerUid'
            ]);
        }
    
        function safeTenantEvidenceUpdate() {
          return signedIn() &&
            // Reject unrelated mutations before database reads and array invariants.
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'photos',
              'primaryPhotoUrl',
              'tenantPhotos',
              'evidenceStatus',
              'evidenceUploadedAt',
              'evidenceUploadError',
              'updatedAt'
            ]) &&
            isNotSuspended() &&
            resource.data.get('evidenceStatus', '') != 'TENANT_EVIDENCE_UPLOADED' &&
            (!('evidenceStatus' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.evidenceStatus in ['PENDING_TENANT_UPLOAD', 'TENANT_EVIDENCE_UPLOADED', 'TENANT_EVIDENCE_UPLOAD_FAILED']) &&
            request.resource.data.get('photos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('photos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('photos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('photos', [])) &&
            request.resource.data.get('tenantPhotos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('tenantPhotos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('tenantPhotos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('tenantPhotos', [])) &&
            (
              resource.data.get('primaryPhotoUrl', '') == '' ||
              request.resource.data.get('primaryPhotoUrl', '') == resource.data.get('primaryPhotoUrl', '')
            );
        }
    
    
    
        function canCreateTenantBoundTicket(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            claimedRole() == 'tenant' &&
            tenantOwns(data) &&
            isNotSuspended() &&
            data.keys().hasAll(['unitId', 'propertyId']) &&
            data.get('unitId', null) is string &&
            data.get('propertyId', null) is string &&
            data.get('unitId', '').size() > 0 &&
            isUnitTenant(data.get('unitId', null)) &&
            data.get('propertyId', null) == get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(data.get('unitId', null))).data.get('propertyId', null) &&
            (!('ownerId' in data) || data.get('ownerId', null) == get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(data.get('unitId', null))).data.get('ownerId', null)) &&
            (!('ownerUid' in data) || data.get('ownerUid', null) == get(/databases/$(database)/documents/units/$(data.get('unitId', null))).data.get('ownerUid', null)) &&
            data.get('source', '') == 'TENANT_PORTAL' &&
            data.get('status', '') == 'OPEN' &&
            data.get('evidenceStatus', '') == 'PENDING_TENANT_UPLOAD' &&
            data.get('assignedTechnicianId', null) == null &&
            data.get('technicianId', null) == null &&
            !data.keys().hasAny([
              'paymentStatus',
              'paymentVerified',
              'adminApproved',
              'approved',
              'estimatedCost',
              'slaCredit',
              'completedAt',
              'closedAt',
              'acceptedAt',
              'arrivedAt',
              'startedAt'
            ]);
        }
    
        function safeDispatcherTicketUpdate() {
          // Non-dispatch callers and unrelated mutations fail before database reads.
          return hasNonAdminDispatchClaimOnly() &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'assignedTechnicianId',
              'technicianId',
              'techId',
              'status',
              'dispatchStatus',
              'assignedAt',
              'autoAssignedAt',
              'reassignedAt',
              'assignmentSource',
              'reassignmentReason',
              'assignedBy',
              'assignedByRole',
              'updatedAt'
            ]) &&
            isNotSuspended() &&
            request.resource.data.get('status', resource.data.get('status', '')) in [
              'OPEN',
              'open',
              'PENDING_ASSIGNMENT',
              'pending_assignment',
              'EMERGENCY_SUBMITTED',
              'emergency_submitted',
              'ASSIGNED',
              'assigned',
              'AUTO_ASSIGNED',
              'auto_assigned',
              'REASSIGNED',
              'reassigned'
            ];
        }
    
        function safeTechnicianTicketUpdate() {
          // The outer allow rule already verifies the technician claim and current
          // assignment. Keep this helper bounded to mutable evidence fields,
          // suspension, dedicated-profile approval, final-state protection, and
          // append-only completion proof.
          return request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'updatedAt',
              'technicianNotes',
              'techNotes',
              'workNotes',
              'notes',
              'afterPhotoUrl',
              'proofPhotos',
              'completionPhotos',
              'evidencePhotos',
              'evidenceStatus',
              'resolutionSummary',
              'materialsUsed',
              'partsDisposition',
              'proofReadiness',
              'arrivedLocation',
              'technicianLocation',
              'technicianLocationUpdatedAt'
            ]) &&
            isNotSuspended() &&
            hasApprovedTechnicianRecord() &&
            !(resource.data.get('status', '') in ['COMPLETED', 'completed', 'CLOSED', 'closed', 'TENANT_APPROVED']) &&
            request.resource.data.get('proofPhotos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('proofPhotos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('proofPhotos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('proofPhotos', [])) &&
            request.resource.data.get('completionPhotos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('completionPhotos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('completionPhotos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('completionPhotos', [])) &&
            request.resource.data.get('evidencePhotos', []).size() >= resource.data.get('evidencePhotos', []).size() &&
            request.resource.data.get('evidencePhotos', []).hasAll(resource.data.get('evidencePhotos', [])) &&
            (
              resource.data.get('afterPhotoUrl', '') == '' ||
              request.resource.data.get('afterPhotoUrl', '') == resource.data.get('afterPhotoUrl', '')
            );
        }
    
        function safeTechnicianProfileUpdate(techId) {
          return signedIn() && request.auth.uid == techId &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'currentLocation',
              'lastLocation',
              'locationUpdatedAt',
              'activeTicketId',
              'isTracking',
              'lastSeenAt',
              'updatedAt'
            ]);
        }
    
        function safeBrokerLeadCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            data.get('brokerId', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            !data.keys().hasAny([
              'commissionEligible',
              'commissionStatus',
              'commissionRate',
              'commissionAmount',
              'commissionCreationStatus',
              'convertedAt',
              'requiresAdminAttribution',
              'adminAttributionRequiredAt',
              'approved',
              'approvedAt',
              'paidAt'
            ]);
        }
    
        function safeBrokerLeadUpdate() {
          return signedIn() &&
            resource.data.get('brokerId', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            request.resource.data.get('brokerId', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'status',
              'lifecycleStatus',
              'notes',
              'attributionId',
              'sourceLeadId',
              'updatedAt'
            ]) &&
            (!('status' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.status in [
              'new',
              'contacted',
              'viewing',
              'negotiation',
              'rejected'
            ]);
        }
    
        function safeBrokerReferralCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            data.get('brokerId', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            !data.keys().hasAny([
              'commissionStatus',
              'commissionRate',
              'commissionAmount',
              'commissionCreationStatus',
              'approved',
              'approvedAt',
              'paidAt'
            ]);
        }
    
        function safeBrokerReferralUpdate() {
          return signedIn() &&
            resource.data.get('brokerId', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            request.resource.data.get('brokerId', null) == request.auth.uid &&
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'notes',
              'updatedAt'
            ]);
        }
    
        // Direct client open-mission claims are denied. Assignment requires
        // canDispatchJobs() / Admin SDK or the acceptTechnicianTicket transaction.
        function safeParticipantDocumentCreate(data) {
          return signedIn() &&
            (owns(data) || tenantOwns(data)) &&
            (!('propertyId' in data) || isPropertyOwner(data.get('propertyId', null)) || getTenantPropertyId() == data.get('propertyId', null)) &&
            (!('verified' in data) || data.verified == false) &&
            (!('approved' in data) || data.approved == false) &&
            (!('adminApproved' in data) || data.adminApproved == false) &&
            (!('status' in data) || data.status in ['PENDING', 'pending', 'PENDING_VERIFICATION', 'pending_verification', 'UPLOADED']);
        }
    
        function canAccessMaintenanceTicket(ticketId) {
          return signedIn() && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/maintenanceTickets/$(ticketId)) && participantCanRead(get(/databases/$(database)/documents/maintenanceTickets/$(ticketId)).data);
        }
    
        function safeTicketMessageCreate(ticketId) {
          return canAccessMaintenanceTicket(ticketId) &&
            (!('senderId' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.senderId == request.auth.uid) &&
            (!('userId' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid) &&
            (!('createdBy' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.createdBy == request.auth.uid);
        }
    
        function ownerBankOwner(data) {
          return signedIn() && (
            data.get('ownerId', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            data.get('ownerUid', null) == request.auth.uid ||
            emailMatches(data.get('ownerEmail', null))
          );
        }
    
        function safeOwnerBankAccountCreate(data) {
          return ownerBankOwner(data) &&
            data.keys().hasOnly([

              'iban',
              'maskedIban',
              'last4',

              'accountHolder',
              'accountHolderName',
              'verificationStatus',
              'status',
              'verified',
              'approved',
              'createdAt',
              'updatedAt'
            ]) &&
            (!('verified' in data) || data.verified == false) &&
            (!('approved' in data) || data.approved == false) &&
            (!('verifiedAt' in data)) &&
            (!('verifiedBy' in data)) &&
            (!('approvedAt' in data)) &&
            (!('approvedBy' in data)) &&
            (!('status' in data) || data.status in ['PENDING_VERIFICATION', 'PENDING', 'SUBMITTED']) &&
            (!('verificationStatus' in data) || data.verificationStatus in ['PENDING', 'PENDING_VERIFICATION']);
        }
    
        function safeOwnerBankAccountUpdate() {

              'iban',
              'maskedIban',
              'last4',
              'bankName',

              'accountHolderName',
              'verificationStatus',
              'status',
              'updatedAt'
            ]) &&
            (!('verified' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.verified == resource.data.get('verified', null)) &&
            (!('approved' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.approved == resource.data.get('approved', null)) &&
            (!('verifiedAt' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.verifiedAt == resource.data.get('verifiedAt', null)) &&
            (!('verifiedBy' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.verifiedBy == resource.data.get('verifiedBy', null)) &&
            (!('approvedAt' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.approvedAt == resource.data.get('approvedAt', null)) &&
            (!('approvedBy' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.approvedBy == resource.data.get('approvedBy', null)) &&

            ]) &&
